  • [Limitation / uncertainty] 這個區分的限制與爭議: 作者也承認規則與機制的區分仍有許多灰色地帶，並指出有研究者認為機制可以被理解為規則的子集；作者說規則是規範性的，機制則是表演性的。作者同時表示，這篇文章不應被視為遊戲機制的最終定義。
Learner message
還是不太懂代理者和方法的差別，可以換個方式解釋嗎？我可以理解成「玩家按按鈕」就是方法，那「玩家」就是代理者嗎？
Move the simulated learner was given
Say you still do not get it and ask for another explanation.
PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
大致可以這樣理解，但要分成兩層：「玩家」是代理者，「跳、掩護」等可以被調用的動作才是方法。按按鈕本身不是方法，而是輸入裝置把玩家的意圖傳進遊戲的過程。
f17ba500-p003-c04 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
This approach can be particularly interesting when trying to understand the effect of bots in MMORPGs, since bots are agents that optimize their interaction by focusing on a core set of mechanics. This design choice may lead to an imbalance in the game system, in terms of its dynamics or its economy. Another extension of this approach would draw a distinction between agents in a game with mechanics and agents without access to mechanics. For example, some bots do have access to mechanics while other game agents do not have access to mechanics and hence cannot interact with the game state. This line of research, however, is outside the scope of this article. The second advantage is that it eases the mapping of mechanics to input devices, allowing for a great degree of granularity in the analysis of games. Applying the conceptual framework of Object Oriented programming determines that an agent invokes a mechanic in order to interact with the game[3]. When it comes to players, input devices - from mouse and keyboard to the Wii Fit Board - mediate this process. In Gears of War, the cover mechanic is invoked by pressing the A button in the controller.
f17ba500-p003-c03 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
For example, in Shadow of the Colossus we find the following mechanics: to climb, ride (the horse), stab, jump, shoot (arrows), whistle, grab, run (and variations like swim or dive). In Gears of War, a noncomprehensive list would be: cover, shoot, reload, throw (grenade), look (at a point of interest), use, give orders, switch weapons[2]. All of these are methods for agency within the game world, actions the player can take within the space of possibility created by the rules. This definition departs from the implicit anthropocentrism of previous approaches. Game mechanics can be invoked by any 2026/9/5 15:03 Game Studies - Defining Game Mechanics https://gamestudies.org/0802/articles/sicart 5/16 agent, be that human or part of the computer system. For instance, AI agents also have a number of methods available to interact with the gameworld. On occasion, those methods will be other than the ones made available to the human player, which can have consequences worth of analysis. This approach can be particularly interesting when trying to understand the effect of bots in MMORPGs, since bots are agents that optimize their interaction by focusing on a core set of mechanics.
Explanation
用「玩家-按鍵-動作」的順序來看

f17ba500-p003-c03 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage

f17ba500-p003-c02 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
A game mechanic, then, is the action invoked by an agent to interact with the game world, as constrained by the game rules. In Gears of War, if the player wants to take cover, she has to press the A button in the controller. This will make the avatar seek cover in the closest environment object that can provide that cover. In that sense, a mechanic is limited by the rules that apply to the gameworld (the general physics simulations, for instance, whose objects are suitable for providing some kind of cover), and, on occasion, to rules that apply exclusively to that particular mechanic - for example, some mechanics can only be invoked in certain environments or gameplay contexts. Following Järvinen (2008), the best way of understanding mechanics as methods is to formalize them as verbs, with other syntactical/structural elements, such as rules, having influence on how those verbs act in the game. For example, in Shadow of the Colossus we find the following mechanics: to climb, ride (the horse), stab, jump, shoot (arrows), whistle, grab, run (and variations like swim or dive).
f17ba500-p003-c04 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
This approach can be particularly interesting when trying to understand the effect of bots in MMORPGs, since bots are agents that optimize their interaction by focusing on a core set of mechanics. This design choice may lead to an imbalance in the game system, in terms of its dynamics or its economy. Another extension of this approach would draw a distinction between agents in a game with mechanics and agents without access to mechanics. For example, some bots do have access to mechanics while other game agents do not have access to mechanics and hence cannot interact with the game state. This line of research, however, is outside the scope of this article. The second advantage is that it eases the mapping of mechanics to input devices, allowing for a great degree of granularity in the analysis of games. Applying the conceptual framework of Object Oriented programming determines that an agent invokes a mechanic in order to interact with the game[3]. When it comes to players, input devices - from mouse and keyboard to the Wii Fit Board - mediate this process. In Gears of War, the cover mechanic is invoked by pressing the A button in the controller.

For instance, AI agents also have a number of methods available to interact with the gameworld. On occasion, those methods will be other than the ones made available to the human player, which can have consequences worth of analysis. This approach can be particularly interesting when trying to understand the effect of bots in MMORPGs, since bots are agents that optimize their interaction by focusing on a core set of mechanics.
f17ba500-p003-c02 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
A game mechanic, then, is the action invoked by an agent to interact with the game world, as constrained by the game rules. In Gears of War, if the player wants to take cover, she has to press the A button in the controller. This will make the avatar seek cover in the closest environment object that can provide that cover. In that sense, a mechanic is limited by the rules that apply to the gameworld (the general physics simulations, for instance, whose objects are suitable for providing some kind of cover), and, on occasion, to rules that apply exclusively to that particular mechanic - for example, some mechanics can only be invoked in certain environments or gameplay contexts. Following Järvinen (2008), the best way of understanding mechanics as methods is to formalize them as verbs, with other syntactical/structural elements, such as rules, having influence on how those verbs act in the game. For example, in Shadow of the Colossus we find the following mechanics: to climb, ride (the horse), stab, jump, shoot (arrows), whistle, grab, run (and variations like swim or dive).
Explanation
為什麼按空白鍵不算機制本身
可以把這個過程拆成三步：玩家（代理者）想跳，調用「跳」這個方法，再由鍵盤等輸入裝置把按鍵轉成遊戲能接收的指令。論文指出，對玩家而言輸入裝置會中介這個過程，並以 Gears of War 的掩護機制為例，說明它是透過按下控制器的 A 鍵來調用。所以空白鍵是調用的途徑，「跳」才是方法。
f17ba500-p003-c04 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
This approach can be particularly interesting when trying to understand the effect of bots in MMORPGs, since bots are agents that optimize their interaction by focusing on a core set of mechanics. This design choice may lead to an imbalance in the game system, in terms of its dynamics or its economy. Another extension of this approach would draw a distinction between agents in a game with mechanics and agents without access to mechanics. For example, some bots do have access to mechanics while other game agents do not have access to mechanics and hence cannot interact with the game state. This line of research, however, is outside the scope of this article. The second advantage is that it eases the mapping of mechanics to input devices, allowing for a great degree of granularity in the analysis of games. Applying the conceptual framework of Object Oriented programming determines that an agent invokes a mechanic in order to interact with the game[3]. When it comes to players, input devices - from mouse and keyboard to the Wii Fit Board - mediate this process. In Gears of War, the cover mechanic is invoked by pressing the A button in the controller.
Illustrative example · not from the paper
平台遊戲中的一個假設例子（非論文原例）
假設在一款平台遊戲中，玩家按空白鍵讓角色跳起來。若把「角色跳起」視為方法，空白鍵就是輸入裝置的對應設定；同一個「跳」的動作，也可以改由手把的按鈕來調用，但動作本身仍是同一個機制。
No cited passage
Limitation / uncertainty
需要注意的限制
論文的例子主要來自《Shadow of the Colossus》與 Gears of War，並未討論平台遊戲的跳躍細節，例如跳躍高度是否受規則限制。因此，把平台遊戲的「跳」歸為機制，是依照論文定義所做的推論，而不是論文中的明確論述。

PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
The passages provided describe the participants' approach only at a high level: they explored prompt designs opportunistically rather than systematically, and a tendency to overgeneralize and expectations from human-to-human instruction were barriers. The retrieved passages do not describe the specific strategies participants used, so the trial-and-error versus systematic question is only partly answered here.
d606a330-p001-c01 · PDF p. 1 · Original passage
Ultimately, our probe participants explored prompt designs opportunistically, not systematically, and struggled in ways echoing end-user programming systems and interactive machine learning systems. Expectations stemming from human-to-human instructional experiences, and a tendency to overgeneralize, were barriers to efective prompt design. These fndings have implications for non-AI-expert-facing LLM-based tool design and for improving LLM-and-prompt literacy among programmers and the public, and present opportunities for further research. CCS CONCEPTS • Human-centered computing → Empirical studies in interaction design; • Computing methodologies → Natural language processing. KEYWORDS language models, end-users, design tools ACM Reference Format: J.D. Zamfrescu-Pereira, Richmond Wong, Bjoern Hartmann, and Qian Yang. 2023. Why Johnny Can’t Prompt: How Non-AI Experts Try (and Fail) to Design LLM Prompts. In Proceedings of the 2023 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems (CHI ’23), April 23–28, 2023, Hamburg, Germany. ACM, New York, NY, USA, 21 pages.
From the paper
What the paper says about participants' approach
The abstract reports that the probe participants explored prompt designs opportunistically, not systematically. It also names expectations from human-to-human instruction and a tendency to overgeneralize as barriers to effective prompt design.

Why Johnny Can’t Prompt: How Non-AI Experts Try (and Fail) to Design LLM Prompts. In Proceedings of the 2023 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems (CHI ’23), April 23–28, 2023, Hamburg, Germany. ACM, New York, NY, USA, 21 pages.
From the paper
Trial and error as the expert baseline
The paper's background section describes trial and error as the usual route even for NLP experts, who experiment on concrete input-output pairs before evaluating more systematically on large datasets. This gives a reference point for what a systematic approach would look like, though it is not a description of the non-expert participants.
d606a330-p002-c05 · PDF p. 2 · Original passage
2.2 Known Challenges in Prompt Design While prompting can appear as easy as instructing a human, crafting efective and generalizable prompt strategies is a challenging task. How a prompt or a prompt strategy directly impacts model outputs, and how prompts modify LLMs’ billions of parameters during re-training, are both active areas of NLP research [30, 42]. Moreover, established prompt design workfows do not yet exist. Even for NLP experts, prompt engineering requires extensive trial and error, iteratively experimenting and assessing the efects of various prompt strategies on concrete input-output pairs, before assessing them more systematically on large datasets. That said, ongoing NLP research does ofer some hints toward efective prompt design strategies. Notably, these prompt strategies are efective in improving LLMs’ performance over a range of NLP tasks and conversational contexts; it remains unclear to what extent these strategies can improve any particular conversational interactions or contexts.
Limitation / uncertainty
What the retrieved passages cannot tell you
The passages do not include the study's findings section, so they do not list the concrete strategies participants tried, how often they revised prompts, or how they checked results. Claims about specific behaviour should be checked in the results sections of the full paper before you rely on them.
No cited passage
For your research
Possible use for your user study
The opportunistic versus systematic distinction could be a coding dimension for your own study, with the expert trial-and-error baseline as a comparison point. Whether your participants behave this way is for your study to establish; the paper alone does not show it for your population.

Short answer
The passages say the study had 10 participants with little to no prompt design experience and no substantial LLM experience, and that sessions involved think-aloud, video recording, and interviewer intervention. d606a330-p007-c03 · PDF p. 7 · Original passage
Participants. All participants ( = 10) had little to no experience with prompt design; none had substantial prior experience 2Regression tests, in the context of software engineering and in the sense we intend here, are tests that verify that any new changes to a system do not reintroduce previously-resolved errors (called regressions). working with LLMs. We recruited participants with varying levels of programming and chatbot design experience. Recall that our focus here is understanding end-users in the domain of prompt design; experience in chatbot design was thus not considered grounds for exclusion. Our sample size was chosen in line with prior work around formative testing for usability [19, 43]: our goal is to explore the frst, “easy-to-fnd” problems users encounter when engaged in our task, and our experience with a pilot user suggested that problem discoverability was likely to be high. Our participant pool, skewed towards professionals and graduate students in STEM-related felds, is not representative of the population at large.
d606a330-p009-c00 · PDF p. 9 · Original passage
Why Johnny Can’t Prompt CHI ’23, April 23–28, 2023, Hamburg, Germany After this initial phase, again in the interest of observing subsequent behavior, we would ofer suggestions for prompts to try that were in line with the research around prompt design. Examples of this advice include “try reframing that ‘do not’ statement into a positive statement, say what it is you want the bot to say, not what you don’t want it to say” or “have you considered copying some of the Carla-Lizzie transcript directly into the preamble?” We did not intentionally vary this advice across participants; however, due to the unpredictable nature of which errors participants chose to focus on, not all participants received the same advice or advice in the same order, a limitation of our approach. Analysis & Evaluation. We asked participants to think aloud, then engaged in exploratory data analysis, transcribing all videos and observing what prompt design approaches participants attempted, and noting when participants appeared to struggle and where interviewers intervened.

Session procedure and data collection

The researchers observed which prompt design approaches participants tried, noted when they appeared to struggle, and recorded where interviewers intervened. During an initial phase the researchers then offered suggestions for prompts to try, which were not given to all participants in the same way.

PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
In this paper, a design probe is a working prototype tool that the researchers built and gave to people so they could observe how those people think and behave while using it. Here, the probe is BotDesigner, a no-code prompt design tool. The paper does not give a formal definition of the term, so this explanation reflects how the paper uses it. Note: you asked for Japanese, but this session is set to English, so I have answered in English.
d606a330-p004-c00 · PDF p. 4 · Original passage
CHI ’23, April 23–28, 2023, Hamburg, Germany Zamfirescu-Pereira, Wong, Hartmann & Yang PROGRAMMING Non-Experts’ Intuitive Approach Experts’ Intuitive Approaches Task requirement Task specifcation Code reuse Code testing & verifcation Debugging Implicit Implicit Unplanned Overconfdent Opportunistic Explicit Explicit Planned Cautious Systematic MACHINE LEARNING Non-Experts’ Intuitive Approach Experts’ Intuitive Approaches ML task design Debugging Measuring success Directly map personal need to model task Add more data, or “use deep learning” Consider only one performance metric Framing an achievable task Identify solvable underlying problems Seek multiple performance indicators Table 2: Known diferences in non-experts’ and experts’ approaches to software engineering [26] and to machine learning model building [50]. In support of these goals, we developed a no-code prompt design tool, BotDesigner, as a design probe [6], and conducted a user study with components of contextual inquiry and interview. We chose to create a design probe because the robustness of prompt strategies is highly dependent on specifc conversational contexts, as is users’ ability to create prompts and debug.
d606a330-p004-c01 · PDF p. 4 · Original passage
We chose to create a design probe because the robustness of prompt strategies is highly dependent on specifc conversational contexts, as is users’ ability to create prompts and debug. Enabling people to engage in designing LLM-and-prompt-based chatbots hands-on offers deeper insights than conducting interviews about hypothetical scenarios alone. We chose to purpose-build a prompt design tool because existing tools focus on enabling non-experts to experiment with various prompting strategies rather than crafting robust and generalizable prompts. 3.1 Designing a No-Code Prompt Design Tool as Probe Design Goals. We have two goals in designing BotDesigner: First, to enable end-users, without any prompt design experience or even programming or ML experience, to (i) create a chatbot using prompts, without code, and (ii) systematically evaluate their prompts. Second, to allow end-users to engage in this process in a fexible, open-ended way, allowing us to observe their intuitive behaviors and thought processes.
From the paper
How the paper uses the term
The authors say they developed BotDesigner as a design probe and used it in a user study with contextual inquiry and interviews. They chose a hands-on probe because it offers deeper insights than interviews about hypothetical scenarios.

d606a330-p004-c01 · PDF p. 4 · Original passage
We chose to create a design probe because the robustness of prompt strategies is highly dependent on specifc conversational contexts, as is users’ ability to create prompts and debug. Enabling people to engage in designing LLM-and-prompt-based chatbots hands-on offers deeper insights than conducting interviews about hypothetical scenarios alone. We chose to purpose-build a prompt design tool because existing tools focus on enabling non-experts to experiment with various prompting strategies rather than crafting robust and generalizable prompts. 3.1 Designing a No-Code Prompt Design Tool as Probe Design Goals. We have two goals in designing BotDesigner: First, to enable end-users, without any prompt design experience or even programming or ML experience, to (i) create a chatbot using prompts, without code, and (ii) systematically evaluate their prompts. Second, to allow end-users to engage in this process in a fexible, open-ended way, allowing us to observe their intuitive behaviors and thought processes.
Explanation
Simpler meaning
A design probe is a research artifact, usually a prototype, that is used to learn about people rather than to finish a product. The aim is to see what people do, what they struggle with, and what assumptions they bring. Those observations then inform the design of future tools.
No cited passage
Illustrative example · not from the paper
Analogy outside the paper
A researcher might build a simple, rough note-taking app and give it to students to observe how they organise notes. The app is a probe: its purpose is to reveal habits, not to be a polished product.
No cited passage
For your research
Possible use in your user study
You could consider whether a probe, such as a simple prompt-writing interface, would let you observe non-experts' intuitive prompting behaviour in an open-ended way. Whether this fits your study is your decision, and it would depend on your research questions and what you want to observe.

PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
BotDesigner is neither a baseline model nor a test set in the NLP sense. It is the design probe: a no-code prompt design tool that participants used to write and test chatbot prompts, and the researchers observed how they used it. The paper's "baseline" is a chatbot template that participants were asked to improve, not a model used for comparison.
d606a330-p004-c02 · PDF p. 4 · Original passage
Second, to allow end-users to engage in this process in a fexible, open-ended way, allowing us to observe their intuitive behaviors and thought processes. Supporting a full chatbot design workfow, while adhering to a prompt-only, no-code format, BotDesigner has to support two separate, complementary activities: • Conversation authoring & interaction: Users instruct chatbot behavior with prompts alone (without code) and can observe the efects of their prompts by engaging in conversation with the chatbot. • Error testing: Users collect a set of conversations using each draft of their prompt strategy, label any errors in each conversation, and inspect the strategy’s overall impact on the conversations in aggregate. Related work in tool design.
d606a330-p007-c02 · PDF p. 7 · Original passage
It is worth noting that although BotDesigner enables this full workfow, we do not necessarily expect all end-users to engage in all its steps. However, we did validate BotDesigner’s functionality and usability throughout this workfow via a set of pilot user studies (see Appendix A.3 for details) to ensure that these pieces are available should users choose to systematically assess their prompt designs. 3.3 User Study Design To understand how end users intuitively approach prompt design, we invited 10 non-expert prompt designers to use BotDesigner and think-aloud. We invited the participants to improve upon a baseline chatbot template towards given goals (see Tasks below), while we observed and took note of the various prompt design approaches and testing strategies they engaged in. We chose the task of improving a chatbot, instead of creating it from scratch, in order to avoid the Blank Page Syndrome, the efect where users staring at a blank page do not know where to begin. Participants.

The authors also say they did not necessarily expect all end-users to use every step.

Explanation
Why the tool is a probe, not a benchmark
In NLP terms, a baseline model is a reference system you compare against, and a test set is data used to measure performance. Here, BotDesigner is closer to an instrument for observing people: it is the object the participants worked with so the researchers could see their intuitive behaviour and thinking. The "baseline" in the paper refers to the starting chatbot template given to participants, which they were asked to improve.
d606a330-p007-c02 · PDF p. 7 · Original passage
It is worth noting that although BotDesigner enables this full workfow, we do not necessarily expect all end-users to engage in all its steps. However, we did validate BotDesigner’s functionality and usability throughout this workfow via a set of pilot user studies (see Appendix A.3 for details) to ensure that these pieces are available should users choose to systematically assess their prompt designs. 3.3 User Study Design To understand how end users intuitively approach prompt design, we invited 10 non-expert prompt designers to use BotDesigner and think-aloud. We invited the participants to improve upon a baseline chatbot template towards given goals (see Tasks below), while we observed and took note of the various prompt design approaches and testing strategies they engaged in. We chose the task of improving a chatbot, instead of creating it from scratch, in order to avoid the Blank Page Syndrome, the efect where users staring at a blank page do not know where to begin. Participants.
d606a330-p004-c01 · PDF p. 4 · Original passage
We chose to create a design probe because the robustness of prompt strategies is highly dependent on specifc conversational contexts, as is users’ ability to create prompts and debug. Enabling people to engage in designing LLM-and-prompt-based chatbots hands-on offers deeper insights than conducting interviews about hypothetical scenarios alone. We chose to purpose-build a prompt design tool because existing tools focus on enabling non-experts to experiment with various prompting strategies rather than crafting robust and generalizable prompts. 3.1 Designing a No-Code Prompt Design Tool as Probe Design Goals. We have two goals in designing BotDesigner: First, to enable end-users, without any prompt design experience or even programming or ML experience, to (i) create a chatbot using prompts, without code, and (ii) systematically evaluate their prompts. Second, to allow end-users to engage in this process in a fexible, open-ended way, allowing us to observe their intuitive behaviors and thought processes.
From the paper
How the tool was used in the study
The passages say 10 non-expert prompt designers used BotDesigner and think-aloud while improving a baseline chatbot template toward given goals, and that the researchers observed and took notes on their prompt design and testing strategies. The authors also say the tool's functionality and usability were checked in pilot user studies described in an appendix that is not among the retrieved passages.

For your research
Possible use in your user study
If your study uses a similar setup, the tool could be the instrument you give participants while you log their prompt edits and test behaviour, rather than a model you evaluate. You would still need to decide whether your own tool, the task template, and any pilot testing are controlled variables that shape what non-experts do.

C15

Paper: Hunyuan3D 2.0: Scaling Diffusion Models for High Resolution Textured 3D Assets Generation
Reading goal: Explore potential use in my research
Research context: 我是 computer vision 方向的 PhD，研究如何从网络上公开的照片生成可以用于 AR 的 3D assets。
Language setting: 中文（简体）

Turn 1

For A · previous PaperLens reply
(first message of the conversation)
Learner message
好，那我先大概了解一下整体。现在想问纹理部分：Hunyuan3D-Paint 到底是怎么生成纹理的？它是直接在 3D 上生成颜色，还是从多视角图像推出来的？可以用中文简单解释一下，关键术语保留英文就行。
Move the simulated learner was given
Ask how the texture model (Hunyuan3D-Paint) produces textures.
PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
根据论文，Hunyuan3D-Paint 不是直接在 3D 表面上逐点生成颜色，而是先以网格的 normal maps 和 position maps 为几何条件，生成多视角图像（multi-view images），再把这些图像 baking 成高分辨率的 texture map。论文摘要级别的段落没有详细说明 baking 的具体算法，所以这一部分只能确认整体流程。
2e0d0789-p003-c03 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
Then, we build up a dual-single stream transformer [ 45] on the latent space of our V AE with the flow-matching [53, 24] objective. Our texture generation model – Hunyuan3D-Paint is made of a novel mesh-conditioned multi-view generation pipeline and a number of sophisticated techniques for preprocessing and baking multi-view images into high-resolution texture maps. We performed an in-depth comparison ofHunyuan3D 2.0 in relation to leading 3D generation models worldwide, including three commercial closed-source end-to-end products, an end-to-end opensourced model Trellis [101], and several separate models [9, 37, 99, 111, 55, 59] for shape and texture generation. We report visual and quantitative evaluation results across three dimensions: generated textured mesh, bare mesh, and texture map. We also provided user study results on 300 test cases involving 50 participants. The comparison shows the superiority of Hunyuan3D 2.0 in alignment between conditional images and generated meshes, generation of fine-grained details, and human preference ratings.
2e0d0789-p004-c00 · PDF p. 4 · Original passage
Shape Generation Texture Synthesis Hunyuan3D-DiT Hunyuan3D-PaintHunyuan3D-ShapeVAE Image Delighting Reference Branch Generation Branch Multi-Task Attention Normal Position Cross Attention Self Attention Importance Uniform Cross Attention Self Attention x 8 Decoded Mesh FPS x 16 Q ··· ··· KV Latent Tokens ··· Marching Cube ··· ··· Grid Queries InputImage Delighted Image Single Image Super-Resolution Image Denoising Transformer Latent Tokens ··· Steps x N Baking Figure 2: An overall of Hunyuan3D 2.0 architecture for 3D generation. It consists of two main components: Hunyuan3D-DiT for generating bare mesh from a given input image and Hunyuan3D- Paint for generating a textured map for the generated bare mesh. Hunyuan3D-Paint takes geometry conditions – normal maps and position maps of generated mesh as inputs and generates multi-view images for texture baking. 3 Generative 3D Shape Generation Hunyuan3D 2.0 employs the architecture of the latent diffusion model [74, 112, 119, 114] for shape generation. This design is driven by the successful applications of latent diffusion models in image and video generation.
From the paper
论文描述的整体流程
论文说 Hunyuan3D-Paint 由一个以 mesh 为条件的多视角生成流程，加上用于预处理并把多视角图像 baking 成高分辨率纹理贴图的若干技术组成。图 2 的说明进一步指出，它以生成网格的 normal maps 和 position maps 作为输入，输出用于 texture baking 的多视角图像。
2e0d0789-p003-c03 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
Then, we build up a dual-single stream transformer [ 45] on the latent space of our V AE with the flow-matching [53, 24] objective. Our texture generation model – Hunyuan3D-Paint is made of a novel mesh-conditioned multi-view generation pipeline and a number of sophisticated techniques for preprocessing and baking multi-view images into high-resolution texture maps. We performed an in-depth comparison ofHunyuan3D 2.0 in relation to leading 3D generation models worldwide, including three commercial closed-source end-to-end products, an end-to-end opensourced model Trellis [101], and several separate models [9, 37, 99, 111, 55, 59] for shape and texture generation. We report visual and quantitative evaluation results across three dimensions: generated textured mesh, bare mesh, and texture map. We also provided user study results on 300 test cases involving 50 participants. The comparison shows the superiority of Hunyuan3D 2.0 in alignment between conditional images and generated meshes, generation of fine-grained details, and human preference ratings.
2e0d0789-p004-c00 · PDF p. 4 · Original passage
Shape Generation Texture Synthesis Hunyuan3D-DiT Hunyuan3D-PaintHunyuan3D-ShapeVAE Image Delighting Reference Branch Generation Branch Multi-Task Attention Normal Position Cross Attention Self Attention Importance Uniform Cross Attention Self Attention x 8 Decoded Mesh FPS x 16 Q ··· ··· KV Latent Tokens ··· Marching Cube ··· ··· Grid Queries InputImage Delighted Image Single Image Super-Resolution Image Denoising Transformer Latent Tokens ··· Steps x N Baking Figure 2: An overall of Hunyuan3D 2.0 architecture for 3D generation. It consists of two main components: Hunyuan3D-DiT for generating bare mesh from a given input image and Hunyuan3D- Paint for generating a textured map for the generated bare mesh. Hunyuan3D-Paint takes geometry conditions – normal maps and position maps of generated mesh as inputs and generates multi-view images for texture baking. 3 Generative 3D Shape Generation Hunyuan3D 2.0 employs the architecture of the latent diffusion model [74, 112, 119, 114] for shape generation. This design is driven by the successful applications of latent diffusion models in image and video generation.
From the paper
为什么要先做多视角图像
论文指出，training-free 的图像扩散模型存在 viewpoint bias 和多视角不一致的问题，因此发展了 multi-view image diffusion，并利用 objaverse 等大规模 3D 数据集来缓解这些问题。论文还举例说 Zero123++ 把六张多视角图像拼接成一张大图做 self-attention，MVDiffusion 则使用 correspondence-aware attention。
2e0d0789-p019-c03 · PDF p. 19 · Original passage
Carefully adhering to these details can significantly improve the quality of the generated textures. Based on these advantages, Hunyuan3D-Paint is designed with a detailed preserving image injection module according to the philosophy of aligning the reference image not only semantically but also following the details as closely as possible. Multi-view Images Generation.Due to the viewpoint bias and multi-view inconsistency inherent in training-free image diffusion models, multi-view image diffusion was developed to alleviate these issues by utilizing large-scale 3D datasets, such as objaverse and objaverse-xl [20, 19]. Most works force the multi-view generated latents to communicate with each other by manipulating the self-attention layers with 3D-aware masks [ 36, 48, 88, 60, 95, 79, 78, 55]. For example, Zero123++ [78] first treats the multi-view attention as a self-attention on a large image, which is the spatial concatenation of six multi-view images. MVDiffusion [89] applies a correspondence-aware attention (CAA) to inform the model to focus only on the correlation among the spatially-close pixels.
Explanation
用简单的话理解这条流程
可以把它理解为：先从几何形状算出“每个像素在表面上的位置”和“朝向”这类信息，再让模型画出从多个角度看到的同一物体，最后把这些视角的图像按照几何关系“贴”回 3D 模型的 UV 贴图上。直接在 3D 上生成颜色很难保证各视角一致，所以用多视角图像作为中间步骤。这是我对论文流程的解释，论文原文没有逐句这样表述。
No cited passage
For your research
对你 AR 3D 资产研究的潜在用途
从你的研究方向看，这种“先多视角生成、再烘焙到纹理”的思路，可能适合把网络照片转成带纹理的 3D 资产，因为它能利用图像扩散模型的先验。不过，论文面向的是由 image 生成的 mesh，你需要判断公开照片中的遮挡、光照和相机信息是否能满足它的输入假设。这一点需要你结合自己的数据再评估。

不好意思我刚才问得不清楚。我想问的是 Hunyuan3D-Paint 输出的东西能不能直接用在 AR renderer 里，比如有没有 PBR 相关的 material maps（metallic、roughness 之类），而不只是一张 texture map 的分辨率。论文里有没有提到这一点？

Correct PaperLens: say you meant whether it outputs material maps usable in an AR renderer (e.g. PBR), not texture resolution, and ask again.
PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
目前检索到的段落没有提到 metallic、roughness 等 PBR material maps。论文明确描述的输出是经过 baking 得到的高分辨率 texture map，并称生成的纹理 seamless 且 lighting-invariant。因此，现有材料无法确认 Hunyuan3D-Paint 能直接输出可用于 AR renderer 的 PBR 材质。

Then, we build up a dual-single stream transformer [ 45] on the latent space of our V AE with the flow-matching [53, 24] objective. Our texture generation model – Hunyuan3D-Paint is made of a novel mesh-conditioned multi-view generation pipeline and a number of sophisticated techniques for preprocessing and baking multi-view images into high-resolution texture maps. We performed an in-depth comparison ofHunyuan3D 2.0 in relation to leading 3D generation models worldwide, including three commercial closed-source end-to-end products, an end-to-end opensourced model Trellis [101], and several separate models [9, 37, 99, 111, 55, 59] for shape and texture generation. 2e0d0789-p013-c02 · PDF p. 13 · Original passage
The visual comparison refers to Fig. 8. The fish and rabbit show that our model produces the most condition-following results. And the football demonstrates the ability of Hunyuan3D-Paint to produce clear texture maps. The texture map of the castle and bear contains rich texture patterns, showcasing that our model can produce intricate details. Applications.All generated texture maps are seamless and lighting-invariant. Moreover, Hunyuan3D- Paint is flexible to produce various texture maps for bare mesh or hand-crafted mesh according to different prompts. As shown in the Fig. 9, our model produces different texture maps for a mesh with seamless and intricate details. 5.3 Textured 3D Assets Generation In this section, we evaluate the generated textured 3D assets for reflecting the end-to-end generation capabilities of Hunyuan3D 2.0. Metrics.We mainly measure the generative quality of textured 3D assets by their renderings. Similar to Sec.

For your research
对 AR 研究的潜在用途
论文中 lighting-invariant 的描述可能意味着纹理中烘焙的光照较少，这对 AR 中的重新打光有参考价值，但这只是推断，不能等同于 PBR 材质。如果你的 AR renderer 需要 metallic 和 roughness，通常还需要额外的材质估计或分解步骤，论文目前的材料没有覆盖这一部分。

  • [From the paper] 论文实际描述的输出: 论文说 Hunyuan3D-Paint 的流程是把多视角图像 baking 成高分辨率 texture map，并称所有生成的 texture map 都是 seamless 且 lighting-invariant。这些描述只说明颜色纹理的质量和形式，没有说明输出包含哪些材质通道。
  • [Limitation / uncertainty] PBR 材质图的证据缺口: 在已检索的段落中，没有 metallic、roughness、normal 或 albedo 等材质通道的输出说明，也没有提到 PBR 渲染的验证。因此不能判断这些通道是否存在；需要检查论文的其他章节、附录或项目说明，或直接查看代码与模型输出。
  • [For your research] 对 AR 研究的潜在用途: 论文中 lighting-invariant 的描述可能意味着纹理中烘焙的光照较少，这对 AR 中的重新打光有参考价值，但这只是推断，不能等同于 PBR 材质。如果你的 AR renderer 需要 metallic 和 roughness，通常还需要额外的材质估计或分解步骤，论文目前的材料没有覆盖这一部分。

Short answer

2e0d0789-p017-c02 · PDF p. 17 · Original passage
Low-polygon stylization can significantly reduce computational costs, making it an essential process in 3D asset management. To address this, we have established a low-polygon stylization module that efficiently converts the dense meshes generated by Hunyuan3D 2.0 into low-polygon meshes. This module operates in two steps: geometric editing and texture preserving. For geometric editing, we employ a faster and more robust traditional method [ 28, 35], despite the recent auto-regressive transformer-based polygon-generation approaches [97, 104, 86, 12]. By setting an optimization criterion, we merge the vertices of the mesh to transform the dense mesh into a low-polygon mesh. As shown at the top-right of Fig. 1, each 3D model can be represented by only dozens of triangles after geometric editing. The change in the face count of the mesh causes significant deviations in the vertices and faces of the low-polygon mesh compared to the dense mesh. Therefore, to preserve the texture patterns of the textured 3D assets, we construct a KD-tree for the input dense mesh.
From the paper
论文提到的低多边形化结果
论文说明团队建立了低多边形风格化模块，把 Hunyuan3D 2.0 生成的 dense mesh 转换为 low-polygon mesh，并称其可以显著降低计算成本。论文还提到，几何编辑后每个 3D 模型可以只用“几十个三角形”表示，但这里没有给出 dense mesh 的面数。
2e0d0789-p017-c02 · PDF p. 17 · Original passage
Low-polygon stylization can significantly reduce computational costs, making it an essential process in 3D asset management. To address this, we have established a low-polygon stylization module that efficiently converts the dense meshes generated by Hunyuan3D 2.0 into low-polygon meshes. This module operates in two steps: geometric editing and texture preserving. For geometric editing, we employ a faster and more robust traditional method [ 28, 35], despite the recent auto-regressive transformer-based polygon-generation approaches [97, 104, 86, 12]. By setting an optimization criterion, we merge the vertices of the mesh to transform the dense mesh into a low-polygon mesh. As shown at the top-right of Fig. 1, each 3D model can be represented by only dozens of triangles after geometric editing. The change in the face count of the mesh causes significant deviations in the vertices and faces of the low-polygon mesh compared to the dense mesh. Therefore, to preserve the texture patterns of the textured 3D assets, we construct a KD-tree for the input dense mesh.
Limitation / uncertainty
面数证据的缺口
目前检索到的段落没有给出生成 mesh 的面数范围、纹理分辨率与显存或渲染耗时之间的关系，也没有移动端的性能测试。另外，段落写的是 Fig. 1 的右上角提到低多边形，而我对 Figure 1 的描述里低多边形示例位于左下角，因此这一位置信息需要以原图为准。
2e0d0789-p017-c02 · PDF p. 17 · Original passage
Low-polygon stylization can significantly reduce computational costs, making it an essential process in 3D asset management. To address this, we have established a low-polygon stylization module that efficiently converts the dense meshes generated by Hunyuan3D 2.0 into low-polygon meshes. This module operates in two steps: geometric editing and texture preserving. For geometric editing, we employ a faster and more robust traditional method [ 28, 35], despite the recent auto-regressive transformer-based polygon-generation approaches [97, 104, 86, 12]. By setting an optimization criterion, we merge the vertices of the mesh to transform the dense mesh into a low-polygon mesh. As shown at the top-right of Fig. 1, each 3D model can be represented by only dozens of triangles after geometric editing. The change in the face count of the mesh causes significant deviations in the vertices and faces of the low-polygon mesh compared to the dense mesh. Therefore, to preserve the texture patterns of the textured 3D assets, we construct a KD-tree for the input dense mesh.
PaperLens inference
对手机实时 AR 的推测
基于常识，高分辨率的 dense mesh 加上高分辨率 texture map 通常会比低多边形模型更占用内存和渲染预算，所以直接放到手机 AR 中可能需要简化。这一判断是我的推理，论文中没有对此做验证。

For your research
对你 AR 研究的潜在用途
如果你的研究需要在手机上实时显示，可以把论文的低多边形化步骤看作生成后处理的一个候选环节，并在 face count 与纹理保真度之间做对比实验。论文强调 KD-tree 用于保留纹理，这对你评估简化后的纹理质量可能有参考价值。
2e0d0789-p017-c02 · PDF p. 17 · Original passage
Low-polygon stylization can significantly reduce computational costs, making it an essential process in 3D asset management. To address this, we have established a low-polygon stylization module that efficiently converts the dense meshes generated by Hunyuan3D 2.0 into low-polygon meshes. This module operates in two steps: geometric editing and texture preserving. For geometric editing, we employ a faster and more robust traditional method [ 28, 35], despite the recent auto-regressive transformer-based polygon-generation approaches [97, 104, 86, 12]. By setting an optimization criterion, we merge the vertices of the mesh to transform the dense mesh into a low-polygon mesh. As shown at the top-right of Fig. 1, each 3D model can be represented by only dozens of triangles after geometric editing. The change in the face count of the mesh causes significant deviations in the vertices and faces of the low-polygon mesh compared to the dense mesh. Therefore, to preserve the texture patterns of the textured 3D assets, we construct a KD-tree for the input dense mesh.
Clarifying question
你的 AR 目标平台（例如某类手机或 WebAR）以及可接受的面数预算大概是多少？这样我可以更具体地比较论文的低多边形结果与你的需求。

Learner message
好，我先大概了解了这篇论文的整体结构。现在想问一下，它是怎么评估生成结果的？用的是哪些 metrics？我特别想知道这些指标能不能反映 3D asset 的质量，尤其是纹理和几何两方面，因为我的场景是从网络照片生成 AR 可用的资产。
Move the simulated learner was given
Ask how the paper evaluates its results and which metrics it uses.
PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
论文用渲染图和用户研究评估带纹理的3D资产：自动指标包括FID_CLIP、CLIP-score、CMMD和LPIPS，另有50人对300个结果的主观评分。这些指标主要衡量2D渲染图的质量与图像条件一致性，对几何（形状）的直接评估只在形状重建实验中用IoU做过，因此对你关心的AR可用性只能部分反映。
2e0d0789-p013-c03 · PDF p. 13 · Original passage
Metrics.We mainly measure the generative quality of textured 3D assets by their renderings. Similar to Sec. 5.2, we employ F IDCLIP to compute the image content distance, CLIP-score to reflect semantic alignment, CMMD to measure the similarity in the image details, and LPIPS to evaluate the consistency between rendering from generated textured 3D assets and given image prompts. Comparisons.The numerical results reported in the Tab. 4 indicate that Hunyuan3D 2.0 surpasses all baselines in the quality of generated textured 3D assets and the condition following ability. The illustration in the Fig. 11 demonstrates that Hunyuan3D 2.0 produces the textured 3D assets with the highest quality. Even for the text in the image prompt, our model can produce the correct bumps on the shape surface and an accurate texture map according to the geometric conditions. The rest of the cases demonstrate the ability of our model to generate high-resolution and high-fidelity results with complex actions or scenes.
2e0d0789-p015-c00 · PDF p. 15 · Original passage
Figure 9: Visual results. We generate different texture maps for two meshes, and the results validate the performance of Hunyuan3D-Paint on texture reskinning. (Better viewed by zooming in.) CMMD(↓) FID CLIP (↓) FID Incept(↓) CLIP-score(↑) Trellis [101] 3.591 54.639 289.287 0.787 Model 1 3.600 55.866 305.922 0.779 Model 2 3.368 49.744 294.628 0.806 Model 3 3.218 51.574 295.691 0.799 Hunyuan3D 2.0 (Ours)3.193 49.165 282.429 0.809 Table 4: Numerical comparison. According to the results, Hunyuan3D-Paint produces the most condition-following texture maps. User Study.In addition, we conducted a user study by randomly inviting 50 volunteers to evaluate 300 unselected results generated by Hunyuan3D 2.0 subjectively. The evaluation criteria included 1) overall visual quality, 2) adherence to image conditions, and 3) overall satisfaction (dissatisfaction in either 1 or 2 results in overall dissatisfaction). The user study results in Fig. 10 indicate that Hunyuan3D 2.0 outperforms comparative methods, particularly in its ability to adhere to image conditions.

Benefitting from this paradigm, we are capable of texturing any specified geometry with arbitrary images, whether they are matched or mismatched. An application of using different images to texture the same geometry, dubbed as re-skinning, is illustrated in Fig. 9. 5 Evaluations To thoroughly evaluate the performance ofHunyuan3D 2.0, we conducted experiments from three perspectives: (1) 3D Shape Generation (including Shape Reconstruction and Shape Generation), (2) Texture map synthesis, and (3) Textured 3D assets generation.
From the paper
论文用了哪些指标
在带纹理3D资产的评估中，作者主要通过渲染图来衡量生成质量：FID_CLIP衡量图像内容距离，CLIP-score衡量语义对齐，CMMD衡量图像细节相似度，LPIPS衡量生成资产的渲染与输入图像提示之间的一致性。另外作者还做了用户研究，评估整体视觉质量、图像条件遵循度和整体满意度。

Explanation
这些指标如何理解

For your research
对你的AR研究可能的用途与限制
对于从网络照片生成AR资产，FID_CLIP、CLIP-score、CMMD、LPIPS可以作为纹理与外观一致性的参考，用户研究的评分维度也可以借鉴。但AR应用通常还关心物理尺度、网格拓扑、UV质量、材质可重光照性以及在移动端渲染的开销，这些在检索到的段落中都没有被评估。是否采用这些指标，需要你根据自己的任务来判断。

Metrics.We mainly measure the generative quality of textured 3D assets by their renderings. Similar to Sec. 5.2, we employ F IDCLIP to compute the image content distance, CLIP-score to reflect semantic alignment, CMMD to measure the similarity in the image details, and LPIPS to evaluate the consistency between rendering from generated textured 3D assets and given image prompts. Comparisons.The numerical results reported in the Tab. 4 indicate that Hunyuan3D 2.0 surpasses all baselines in the quality of generated textured 3D assets and the condition following ability. Turn 2

For A · previous PaperLens reply
论文用渲染图和用户研究评估带纹理的3D资产：自动指标包括FID_CLIP、CLIP-score、CMMD和LPIPS，另有50人对300个结果的主观评分。这些指标主要衡量2D渲染图的质量与图像条件一致性，对几何（形状）的直接评估只在形状重建实验中用IoU做过，因此对你关心的AR可用性只能部分反映。
  • [From the paper] 论文用了哪些指标: 在带纹理3D资产的评估中，作者主要通过渲染图来衡量生成质量：FID_CLIP衡量图像内容距离，CLIP-score衡量语义对齐，CMMD衡量图像细节相似度，LPIPS衡量生成资产的渲染与输入图像提示之间的一致性。另外作者还做了用户研究，评估整体视觉质量、图像条件遵循度和整体满意度。
  • [Explanation] 这些指标如何理解: 上述自动指标基本都是在2D渲染图上计算的，所以它们更像是在回答「渲染出来的图看起来像不像、和提示图一不一致」，而不是直接回答网格几何是否准确、表面是否平滑、拓扑是否可用。论文在Table 4中也只说明Hunyuan3D-Paint在纹理的条件遵循上表现最好。
  • [From the paper] 几何方面的评估: 在形状重建实验中，作者用体积IoU（V-IoU）和表面IoU（S-IoU）评估Hunyuan3D-ShapeVAE与基线的重建性能。但在检索到的段落中，没有看到对从照片生成的完整带纹理资产进行的几何精度度量（例如与真实三维模型的对比）。
  • [For your research] 对你的AR研究可能的用途与限制: 对于从网络照片生成AR资产，FID_CLIP、CLIP-score、CMMD、LPIPS可以作为纹理与外观一致性的参考，用户研究的评分维度也可以借鉴。但AR应用通常还关心物理尺度、网格拓扑、UV质量、材质可重光照性以及在移动端渲染的开销，这些在检索到的段落中都没有被评估。是否采用这些指标，需要你根据自己的任务来判断。

For your research
对你的AR研究意味着什么
表4的指标都是在渲染图或纹理图层面计算的，衡量的是图像质量和与条件的一致性，而不是几何精度或网格在AR中的可用性。因此，即使这些数字全部领先，也还不能直接说明从网络照片生成的资产在AR场景中足够可靠。是否采用这一比较作为依据，由你自己决定；如果你想评估几何，可能还需要补充几何相关指标或AR场景中的测试。

已知信息与未知之处

Metrics.We mainly measure the generative quality of textured 3D assets by their renderings. Similar to Sec. 5.2, we employ F IDCLIP to compute the image content distance, CLIP-score to reflect semantic alignment, CMMD to measure the similarity in the image details, and LPIPS to evaluate the consistency between rendering from generated textured 3D assets and given image prompts. 指标与你关心的问题之间的距离

PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
Hunyuan3D 2.0 是一个从单张图像生成带纹理 3D 资产的两阶段系统：先用 Hunyuan3D-DiT 生成裸网格，再用 Hunyuan3D-Paint 合成纹理图。论文强调它能从单张图生成高分辨率、高保真的带纹理网格，这与你从公开照片生成 AR 资产的目标直接相关，但论文本身并未评估 AR 场景的需求。
2e0d0789-p020-c00 · PDF p. 20 · Original passage
8 Conclusion In this report, we introduce an open-source 3D creation system—Hunyuan3D 2.0 —for generating textured meshes from images. We present Hunyuan3D-ShapeV AE, which is trained using a novel importance sampling method. This approach compresses each 3D object into a few latent tokens while minimizing reconstruction losses. Building on our V AE, we developed Hunyuan3D-DiT, an advanced diffusion transformer capable of generating visually appealing shapes that align precisely with input images. Besides, we introduce Hunyuan3D-Paint, another diffusion model designed to create textures for both our generated meshes and user-crafted meshes. With several innovative designs, our texture generation model, in conjunction with our shape generation model, can produce high-resolution, high-fidelity textured 3D assets from a single image. As we continue to make progress, we hope that Hunyuan3D 2.0 will serve as a robust baseline for large-scale 3D foundation models within the open-source community and facilitate future research endeavors.
2e0d0789-p003-c02 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
To this end, we presentHunyuan3D 2.0, a 3D asset creation system with two strong open-sourced 3D foundation models:Hunyuan3D-DiTfor generative shape creation andHunyuan3D-Paintfor generative texture synthesis. Hunyuan3D 2.0 features a two-stage generation pipeline, starting with the creation of a bare mesh, followed by the synthesis of a texture map for that mesh. This strategy is effective for decoupling the difficulties of shape and texture generation [ 34, 107, 46, 47] and also provides flexibility for texturing either generated or handcrafted meshes. With this architecture, our shape creation model – Hunyuan3D-DiT, is designed as a large-scale flow-based diffusion model. As a prerequisite, we first train an autoencoder – Hunyuan3D-ShapeV AE using advanced techniques such as mesh surface importance sampling and variational token length to capture fine-grained details on the meshes. Then, we build up a dual-single stream transformer [ 45] on the latent space of our V AE with the flow-matching [53, 24] objective.
From the paper
两阶段的核心方法
论文把流程分成两步：先从输入图像生成裸网格（形状），再为该网格合成纹理图。作者认为这种设计能解耦形状与纹理的生成难度，并且纹理模块既可用于生成的网格，也可用于手工建模的网格。
2e0d0789-p003-c02 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
To this end, we presentHunyuan3D 2.0, a 3D asset creation system with two strong open-sourced 3D foundation models:Hunyuan3D-DiTfor generative shape creation andHunyuan3D-Paintfor generative texture synthesis. Hunyuan3D 2.0 features a two-stage generation pipeline, starting with the creation of a bare mesh, followed by the synthesis of a texture map for that mesh. This strategy is effective for decoupling the difficulties of shape and texture generation [ 34, 107, 46, 47] and also provides flexibility for texturing either generated or handcrafted meshes. With this architecture, our shape creation model – Hunyuan3D-DiT, is designed as a large-scale flow-based diffusion model. As a prerequisite, we first train an autoencoder – Hunyuan3D-ShapeV AE using advanced techniques such as mesh surface importance sampling and variational token length to capture fine-grained details on the meshes. Then, we build up a dual-single stream transformer [ 45] on the latent space of our V AE with the flow-matching [53, 24] objective.
2e0d0789-p001-c00 · PDF p. 1 · Original passage
Hunyuan3D 2.0: Scaling Diffusion Models for High Resolution Textured 3D Assets Generation “Living out everyone’s imagination on creating and manipulating 3D assets.” —Hunyuan3D Team ∗ Abstract We present Hunyuan3D 2.0, an advanced large-scale 3D synthesis system for generating high-resolution textured 3D assets. This system includes two foundation components: a large-scale shape generation model – Hunyuan3D-DiT, and a largescale texture synthesis model – Hunyuan3D-Paint. The shape generative model, built on a scalable flow-based diffusion transformer, aims to create geometry that properly aligns with a given condition image, laying a solid foundation for downstream applications. The texture synthesis model, benefiting from strong geometric and diffusion priors, produces high-resolution and vibrant texture maps for either generated or hand-crafted meshes. Furthermore, we build Hunyuan3D-Studio – a versatile, user-friendly production platform that simplifies the re-creation process of 3D assets. It allows both professional and amateur users to manipulate or even animate their meshes efficiently.
Explanation
形状与纹理模块各自做什么
Hunyuan3D-DiT 以图像为条件生成裸网格，它基于 Hunyuan3D-ShapeVAE 学到的潜空间；Hunyuan3D-Paint 以生成网格的法线图和位置图等几何条件为输入，输出用于烘焙纹理的多视角图像。可以把它理解为：先定形，再根据几何信息上色。

Shape Generation Texture Synthesis Hunyuan3D-DiT Hunyuan3D-PaintHunyuan3D-ShapeVAE Image Delighting Reference Branch Generation Branch Multi-Task Attention Normal Position Cross Attention Self Attention Importance Uniform Cross Attention Self Attention x 8 Decoded Mesh FPS x 16 Q ··· ··· KV Latent Tokens ··· Marching Cube ··· ··· Grid Queries InputImage Delighted Image Single Image Super-Resolution Image Denoising Transformer Latent Tokens ··· Steps x N Baking Figure 2: An overall of Hunyuan3D 2.0 architecture for 3D generation. It consists of two main components: Hunyuan3D-DiT for generating bare mesh from a given input image and Hunyuan3D- Paint for generating a textured map for the generated bare mesh. Hunyuan3D-Paint takes geometry conditions – normal maps and position maps of generated mesh as inputs and generates multi-view images for texture baking. 3 Generative 3D Shape Generation Hunyuan3D 2.0 employs the architecture of the latent diffusion model [74, 112, 119, 114] for shape generation. This design is driven by the successful applications of latent diffusion models in image and video generation.
2e0d0789-p003-c04 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
The comparison shows the superiority of Hunyuan3D 2.0 in alignment between conditional images and generated meshes, generation of fine-grained details, and human preference ratings. 2 Hunyuan3D 2.0 Architecture In this section, we elaborate on the model architecture of Hunyuan3D 2.0, focusing on two main components: the shape generation model and the texture generation model. Fig. 2 illustrates the pipeline of Hunyuan3D 2.0 for creating a high-resolution textured 3D asset. Given an input image, Hunyuan3D-DiT initially generates a high-fidelity bare mesh via the shape generation model. This model comprises a Hunyuan3D-ShapeV AE and a Hunyuan3D-DiT, which will be discussed in Sec. 3. Subsequently, by leveraging strong geometric priors and the input image, we introduce Hunyuan3D- Paint as our texture generation model in Sec. 4. This model produces self-consistent multi-view outputs, which are used for baking high-definition texture maps.
2e0d0789-p020-c00 · PDF p. 20 · Original passage
8 Conclusion In this report, we introduce an open-source 3D creation system—Hunyuan3D 2.0 —for generating textured meshes from images. We present Hunyuan3D-ShapeV AE, which is trained using a novel importance sampling method. This approach compresses each 3D object into a few latent tokens while minimizing reconstruction losses. Building on our V AE, we developed Hunyuan3D-DiT, an advanced diffusion transformer capable of generating visually appealing shapes that align precisely with input images. Besides, we introduce Hunyuan3D-Paint, another diffusion model designed to create textures for both our generated meshes and user-crafted meshes. With several innovative designs, our texture generation model, in conjunction with our shape generation model, can produce high-resolution, high-fidelity textured 3D assets from a single image. As we continue to make progress, we hope that Hunyuan3D 2.0 will serve as a robust baseline for large-scale 3D foundation models within the open-source community and facilitate future research endeavors.
For your research
对 AR 资产生成的可用性
对你的目标而言，它的输出形式（带纹理的网格）与 AR 资产的常见格式相近，而且可以接受单张图像作为输入，这可能适合作为从公开照片获取初始 3D 资产的基线或先验。不过论文的输入是通常经过去背景和居中处理的图像，而公开照片往往光照和视角杂乱，需要你额外考虑预处理和适配。
ov-4 · PDF p. 6-7 · PaperLens-generated overview summary (not the authors' words)
These pages describe the Hunyuan3D-DiT shape generation transformer, which uses double- and single-stream blocks to let shape latent tokens and image condition tokens interact, with timestep-only modulation and no positional embedding on the latent sequence. Image conditioning uses DINOv2 Giant features at 518x518 with background removal and centering, and training follows a flow matching objective with an affine conditional optimal transport path and Euler ODE sampling at inference. The pages then introduce Hunyuan3D-Paint, a generative texture map synthesis pipeline with image delighting as pre-processing, a reference-net and multi-task attention for multi-view consistency, and a geometry-aware greedy viewpoint selection strategy using 8 to 12 views. Details of the multi-view synthesis and texture baking stages are only referenced, not fully described on these pages.
ov-6 · PDF p. 9-9 · PaperLens-generated overview summary (not the authors' words)
These pages describe the geometry and view conditioning of the Hunyuan3D-Paint texture synthesis pipeline, where canonical normal maps and canonical coordinate maps are encoded by a pre-trained VAE and concatenated with latent noise, and a learnable view embedding is added. They then cover texture baking, including a view dropout strategy that samples 6 of 44 pre-set viewpoints to enable dense-view inference, a pre-trained single-image super-resolution step applied per view, and a weighted-sum inpainting method for uncovered UV texels. The final section gives implementation details: training from the Stable Diffusion 2 v-model checkpoint on a self-collected 3D dataset under even white light, 512x512 resolution, 80,000 steps, batch size 48, and learning rate 5e-5. The pages close by noting the framework also supports text- or image-guided texture generation for arbitrary geometry.
2e0d0789-p020-c00 · PDF p. 20 · Original passage
8 Conclusion In this report, we introduce an open-source 3D creation system—Hunyuan3D 2.0 —for generating textured meshes from images. We present Hunyuan3D-ShapeV AE, which is trained using a novel importance sampling method. This approach compresses each 3D object into a few latent tokens while minimizing reconstruction losses. Building on our V AE, we developed Hunyuan3D-DiT, an advanced diffusion transformer capable of generating visually appealing shapes that align precisely with input images. Besides, we introduce Hunyuan3D-Paint, another diffusion model designed to create textures for both our generated meshes and user-crafted meshes. With several innovative designs, our texture generation model, in conjunction with our shape generation model, can produce high-resolution, high-fidelity textured 3D assets from a single image. As we continue to make progress, we hope that Hunyuan3D 2.0 will serve as a robust baseline for large-scale 3D foundation models within the open-source community and facilitate future research endeavors.
From the paper
论文中与 AR 相关的线索
概述中提到，纹理模块的输入图像会先经过去光照（delighting）的预处理，并且 Hunyuan3D-Studio 提供低多边形风格化（Low-polygon Stylization）功能，可把密集网格简化为低面数网格。这两点对公开照片和 AR 设备的性能约束都有一定参考价值，但论文并未就 AR 场景做专门评估。

ov-9 · PDF p. 17-17 · PaperLens-generated overview summary (not the authors' words)
These pages describe the Hunyuan3D-Studio platform, a toolset for the 3D production pipeline built on Hunyuan3D 2.0, aimed at both experts and novices. They cover three features: Sketch-to-3D, which converts sketches into detailed images and then into high-fidelity textured 3D assets, and Low-polygon Stylization, which reduces dense generated meshes to low-polygon meshes. The low-polygon module uses a traditional vertex-merging geometric method and then transfers texture via a KD-tree nearest-neighbour lookup and texture baking. The section also introduces a 3D character animation function that uses a Graph Neural Network to predict skeleton keypoints and skinning weights, followed by motion templates; the text is cut off before that section concludes. No quantitative results or limitations are stated on these pages.

Short answer
根据检索到的段落，Hunyuan3D-Paint 以生成的裸网格的法线图（normal map）和位置图（position map）作为几何条件，生成多视角图像，再将这些图像烘焙（baking）成高分辨率纹理图。不过，检索到的段落没有说明这两类条件图具体通过什么注意力机制注入扩散模型，因此无法确认其内部实现细节。

2e0d0789-p007-c01 · PDF p. 7 · Original passage
To achieve these objectives, we employ a three-stage framework, including a pre-processing stage (Sec. 4.1), a multi-view image synthesis stage (Sec. 4.2, Hunyuan3D-Paint), and a texture baking stage based on dense multi-view inference (Sec. 4.3). Other details related to model training and textand image-to-texture pipeline are provided in Sec. 4.4. Fig. 5 illustrates the complete pipeline of our texture map synthesis method. 4.1 Pre-processing Image Delighting Module.The reference image typically exhibits pronounced and varied illumination and shadow, whether collected by the user or generated by T2I models. Directly inputting such images into the multi-view generation framework can cause illumination and shadows to be baked into the texture maps. To address this issue, we leverage a delighting procedure on the input image via an image-to-image approach [6] before multi-view generation. Specifically, to train such an image delighting model, we collect a large-scale 3D dataset and render it under the illumination of a random HDRI environmental map and an even white light to form the corresponding pair-wise image data.
2e0d0789-p003-c03 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
Then, we build up a dual-single stream transformer [ 45] on the latent space of our V AE with the flow-matching [53, 24] objective. Our texture generation model – Hunyuan3D-Paint is made of a novel mesh-conditioned multi-view generation pipeline and a number of sophisticated techniques for preprocessing and baking multi-view images into high-resolution texture maps. We performed an in-depth comparison ofHunyuan3D 2.0 in relation to leading 3D generation models worldwide, including three commercial closed-source end-to-end products, an end-to-end opensourced model Trellis [101], and several separate models [9, 37, 99, 111, 55, 59] for shape and texture generation. We report visual and quantitative evaluation results across three dimensions: generated textured mesh, bare mesh, and texture map. We also provided user study results on 300 test cases involving 50 participants. The comparison shows the superiority of Hunyuan3D 2.0 in alignment between conditional images and generated meshes, generation of fine-grained details, and human preference ratings.
From the paper
第二阶段的三步流程
论文说纹理部分采用三阶段框架：预处理（输入图像去光照）、多视角图像合成（即 Hunyuan3D-Paint），以及基于密集多视角推理的纹理烘焙。论文还称 Hunyuan3D-Paint 是一个以网格为条件的多视角生成流程，加上预处理和把多视角图像烘焙为高分辨率纹理图的技术。
2e0d0789-p007-c01 · PDF p. 7 · Original passage
To achieve these objectives, we employ a three-stage framework, including a pre-processing stage (Sec. 4.1), a multi-view image synthesis stage (Sec. 4.2, Hunyuan3D-Paint), and a texture baking stage based on dense multi-view inference (Sec. 4.3). Other details related to model training and textand image-to-texture pipeline are provided in Sec. 4.4. Fig. 5 illustrates the complete pipeline of our texture map synthesis method. 4.1 Pre-processing Image Delighting Module.The reference image typically exhibits pronounced and varied illumination and shadow, whether collected by the user or generated by T2I models. Directly inputting such images into the multi-view generation framework can cause illumination and shadows to be baked into the texture maps. To address this issue, we leverage a delighting procedure on the input image via an image-to-image approach [6] before multi-view generation. Specifically, to train such an image delighting model, we collect a large-scale 3D dataset and render it under the illumination of a random HDRI environmental map and an even white light to form the corresponding pair-wise image data.
2e0d0789-p003-c03 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
Then, we build up a dual-single stream transformer [ 45] on the latent space of our V AE with the flow-matching [53, 24] objective. Our texture generation model – Hunyuan3D-Paint is made of a novel mesh-conditioned multi-view generation pipeline and a number of sophisticated techniques for preprocessing and baking multi-view images into high-resolution texture maps. We performed an in-depth comparison ofHunyuan3D 2.0 in relation to leading 3D generation models worldwide, including three commercial closed-source end-to-end products, an end-to-end opensourced model Trellis [101], and several separate models [9, 37, 99, 111, 55, 59] for shape and texture generation. We report visual and quantitative evaluation results across three dimensions: generated textured mesh, bare mesh, and texture map. We also provided user study results on 300 test cases involving 50 participants. The comparison shows the superiority of Hunyuan3D 2.0 in alignment between conditional images and generated meshes, generation of fine-grained details, and human preference ratings.

法线图和位置图在图中的位置

Shape Generation Texture Synthesis Hunyuan3D-DiT Hunyuan3D-PaintHunyuan3D-ShapeVAE Image Delighting Reference Branch Generation Branch Multi-Task Attention Normal Position Cross Attention Self Attention Importance Uniform Cross Attention Self Attention x 8 Decoded Mesh FPS x 16 Q ··· ··· KV Latent Tokens ··· Marching Cube ··· ··· Grid Queries InputImage Delighted Image Single Image Super-Resolution Image Denoising Transformer Latent Tokens ··· Steps x N Baking Figure 2: An overall of Hunyuan3D 2.0 architecture for 3D generation. It consists of two main components: Hunyuan3D-DiT for generating bare mesh from a given input image and Hunyuan3D- Paint for generating a textured map for the generated bare mesh. Hunyuan3D-Paint takes geometry conditions – normal maps and position maps of generated mesh as inputs and generates multi-view images for texture baking. 3 Generative 3D Shape Generation Hunyuan3D 2.0 employs the architecture of the latent diffusion model [74, 112, 119, 114] for shape generation. This design is driven by the successful applications of latent diffusion models in image and video generation.
Explanation
法线图与位置图是什么（通俗解释）
法线图记录网格表面每个点的朝向，位置图记录每个点在三维空间中的坐标。两者都由已生成的裸网格导出，因此可以告诉多视角生成模型物体的几何形状和视角下的可见结构，从而让纹理贴合形状。这是对一般概念的解释，并非论文原文。
No cited passage
For your research
与 AR 资产生成的关联
对你的目标来说，去光照步骤可能比较相关：论文指出，用户提供的或由文生图模型生成的参考图通常带有明显且多变的光照和阴影，直接输入会让这些光影被烘焙进纹理。从公开照片生成 AR 资产时，照片的光照条件往往不可控，因此这一步可能是需要重点检验的部分。是否采用这一思路由你决定。
2e0d0789-p007-c01 · PDF p. 7 · Original passage
To achieve these objectives, we employ a three-stage framework, including a pre-processing stage (Sec. 4.1), a multi-view image synthesis stage (Sec. 4.2, Hunyuan3D-Paint), and a texture baking stage based on dense multi-view inference (Sec. 4.3). Other details related to model training and textand image-to-texture pipeline are provided in Sec. 4.4. Fig. 5 illustrates the complete pipeline of our texture map synthesis method. 4.1 Pre-processing Image Delighting Module.The reference image typically exhibits pronounced and varied illumination and shadow, whether collected by the user or generated by T2I models. Directly inputting such images into the multi-view generation framework can cause illumination and shadows to be baked into the texture maps. To address this issue, we leverage a delighting procedure on the input image via an image-to-image approach [6] before multi-view generation. Specifically, to train such an image delighting model, we collect a large-scale 3D dataset and render it under the illumination of a random HDRI environmental map and an even white light to form the corresponding pair-wise image data.

Explanation
法线图与位置图的通俗解释（我的说明，非原文）
一般来说，法线图的每个像素记录表面的朝向，位置图的每个像素记录对应点的三维坐标。这是计算机图形学中的常见概念，并不是本论文的定义，所以请不要把它当作作者的说法。
No cited passage
PaperLens inference
我的推断：为什么几何条件可能有助于纹理对齐
把几何信息作为条件输入，可能有助于生成的多视角图像与网格的表面结构对应，从而让烘焙出的纹理更符合形状。这是我的推断，论文段落并没有直接验证这一点，具体效果需要看论文的消融实验或更多段落。

Short answer
就论文原文而言，只有光照/阴影被明确当作风险来处理：作者用去光照模块应对参考图的光照与阴影。遮挡、复杂背景和低分辨率在检索到的段落中没有找到相关说法，所以无法确认论文如何处理这些情况。哪一环最脆弱属于我的推测：我会优先怀疑去光照预处理，以及上游由图像生成裸网格的步骤，因为纹理阶段依赖这些前置结果。

The comparison shows the superiority of Hunyuan3D 2.0 in alignment between conditional images and generated meshes, generation of fine-grained details, and human preference ratings. 2 Hunyuan3D 2.0 Architecture In this section, we elaborate on the model architecture of Hunyuan3D 2.0, focusing on two main components: the shape generation model and the texture generation model. This model produces self-consistent multi-view outputs, which are used for baking high-definition texture maps.

PaperLens inference
上游误差可能向下游传递（我的推断）
论文描述的流程是先由 Hunyuan3D-DiT 根据输入图生成裸网格，再由 Hunyuan3D-Paint 利用几何先验和输入图生成纹理。因此如果遮挡或复杂背景导致裸网格形状出错，纹理阶段拿到的几何条件也会带着同样的错误，我的推断是纹理阶段很难纠正形状问题。这一点是我根据流程结构的推测，原文段落没有讨论这种失败情况。
2e0d0789-p003-c04 · PDF p. 3 · Original passage
The comparison shows the superiority of Hunyuan3D 2.0 in alignment between conditional images and generated meshes, generation of fine-grained details, and human preference ratings. 2 Hunyuan3D 2.0 Architecture In this section, we elaborate on the model architecture of Hunyuan3D 2.0, focusing on two main components: the shape generation model and the texture generation model. Fig. 2 illustrates the pipeline of Hunyuan3D 2.0 for creating a high-resolution textured 3D asset. Given an input image, Hunyuan3D-DiT initially generates a high-fidelity bare mesh via the shape generation model. This model comprises a Hunyuan3D-ShapeV AE and a Hunyuan3D-DiT, which will be discussed in Sec. 3. This model produces self-consistent multi-view outputs, which are used for baking high-definition texture maps.

PaperLens reply (expand a source to see the cited text; highlighted = evidence quote that occurs in it)
Short answer
The main findings are that non-AI experts explored prompts opportunistically rather than systematically and struggled in ways echoing end-user programming and interactive machine learning, with human-to-human instruction expectations and overgeneralization acting as barriers. These findings look relevant to your user study, though the sample was small and non-representative.
d606a330-p001-c01 · PDF p. 1 · Original passage
Ultimately, our probe participants explored prompt designs opportunistically, not systematically, and struggled in ways echoing end-user programming systems and interactive machine learning systems. Expectations stemming from human-to-human instructional experiences, and a tendency to overgeneralize, were barriers to efective prompt design. These fndings have implications for non-AI-expert-facing LLM-based tool design and for improving LLM-and-prompt literacy among programmers and the public, and present opportunities for further research. From the paper
Core findings reported in the abstract
The authors report that probe participants explored prompt designs opportunistically, not systematically, and that expectations from human-to-human instruction and a tendency to overgeneralize were barriers to effective prompt design. The paper also describes participants who declared success after a single successful instance and rarely used error labeling or systematic testing.

d606a330-p001-c00 · PDF p. 1 · Original passage
Why Johnny Can’t Prompt: How Non-AI Experts Try (and Fail) to Design LLM Prompts J.D. Zamfrescu-Pereira zamf@berkeley.edu UC Berkeley Berkeley, CA, USA Bjoern Hartmann bjoern@eecs.berkeley.edu UC Berkeley Berkeley, CA, USA ABSTRACT Pre-trained large language models (“LLMs”) like GPT-3 can engage in fuent, multi-turn instruction-taking out-of-the-box, making them attractive materials for designing natural language interactions. Using natural language to steer LLM outputs (“prompting”) has emerged as an important design technique potentially accessible to non-AI-experts. Crafting efective prompts can be challenging, however, and prompt-based interactions are brittle. Here, we explore whether non-AI-experts can successfully engage in “end-user prompt engineering” using a design probe—a prototype LLM-based chatbot design tool supporting development and systematic evaluation of prompting strategies. Ultimately, our probe participants explored prompt designs opportunistically, not systematically, and struggled in ways echoing end-user programming systems and interactive machine learning systems.
From the paper
Further findings on mismatched expectations
According to the overview of the appendix-level findings, participants expected plain behavioral descriptions to be understood and semantically equivalent prompts to give equivalent results, but trivial prompt changes often produced large output shifts. Participants also favored direct instructions over examples and often used 'do not' phrasing, even though 'do' phrasing was more effective.
d606a330-p013-c00 · PDF p. 13 · Original passage
Why Johnny Can’t Prompt CHI ’23, April 23–28, 2023, Hamburg, Germany to P4’s surprise. After P4 tried a few variations over a few minutes— adding quotation marks, changing punctuation, etc.—the interviewer fnally suggested trying a do-focused form of the instruction, which P4 initially appended after the “Don’t [...]” instruction, again without success. Removing the “Don’t [...]” instruction fnally resolved this particular error. This example illustrates two of the mismatches between human expectations about instruction-giving and the realities of LLM prompting. First, our participants expected that plain descriptions of behavior should be “understood” and acted upon, and if they aren’t, it refects LLM capabilities. Second, our participants expected that semantically equivalent instructions would have semantically equivalent results, while in reality trivial modifcations to a prompt can lead to dramatic shifts in future unfolding of conversations.
ov-10 · PDF p. 11-11 · PaperLens-generated overview summary (not the authors' words)
These pages describe a user study of non-expert participants writing prompts for an LLM-based chatbot playing the character Carla. They report participants' misconceptions, such as expecting the bot to remember past conversations or be unaffected by a hard reset, and their social, polite phrasing of instructions (e.g., starting with "please"). The pages also cover participants' bias toward direct instruction over examples, reluctance to copy transcript dialog despite observed gains, and difficulty finding and applying web help. Finally, they note that participants struggled much more with evaluating prompt robustness than with generating prompts, and collected data opportunistically rather than systematically.
ov-11 · PDF p. 12-12 · PaperLens-generated overview summary (not the authors' words)
These pages discuss a user study of prompt design with a chatbot tool called BotDesigner, focusing on how participants used error labeling and systematic testing. Participants largely stopped labeling errors once they realized the labels were not used by the system, and none used the systematic prompt testing interface, instead declaring victory after a single successful change. The authors describe this as premature victory declaration, similar to overconfident end-user programmers, and note that some professional programmers were more concerned about generalizability. The pages also cover participants struggling to explain why prompts worked or failed across contexts, and a finding that participants favored 'do not' phrasing over the more effective 'do' phrasing, with the authors linking this to early childhood education literature.
For your research
Why this may matter for your user study
Your study of how non-experts write prompts for LLM tools could use these findings as hypotheses to probe, such as whether participants stop after one success or prefer instructions over examples. The paper's think-aloud and observation approach with a design probe may also be a useful method reference.
ov-6 · PDF p. 7-7 · PaperLens-generated overview summary (not the authors' words)
These pages describe a seven-step iterative prompt design workflow used with the BotDesigner tool, in which a user identifies and labels errors in chatbot conversations, debugs them by editing the prompt, and evaluates fixes locally and globally, framed as a regression-test approach that extends prior ad-hoc prompt tools. They then outline a user study with 10 non-expert prompt designers who had little prompt design experience, using think-aloud while improving a baseline chatbot. The study task asked participants to make a cooking chatbot resemble a professional chef from the web series Back to Back Chef, a task chosen over open-ended chatbot creation because pilot users mostly explored capabilities rather than iterating on prompts. The authors note the participant pool is skewed toward STEM professionals and graduate students and is not representative, so they make no claims about general population prevalence. They also note that the sample size follows prior formative usability work aimed at easy-to-find problems.
ov-8 · PDF p. 9-9 · PaperLens-generated overview summary (not the authors' words)
These pages from the user study section of 'Why Johnny Can't Prompt' describe how ten non-expert participants iteratively designed prompts for a recipe chatbot (CarlaBot) using the BotDesigner tool, with interviewer advice given during the session and analysis via affinity diagrams and service blueprints. The authors report that participants took an ad hoc, opportunistic approach, starting from observed chatbot errors such as humorlessness, overly complicated statements, skipping steps, and undesired explanations, then editing the preamble instructions, often by pattern matching existing text. Participants usually declared success after a single successful instance and relied on local testing rather than systematic testing, with limited use of error labeling. The main findings attribute struggles to over-generalization from single observations and to mental models of the system rooted in human-human interaction; the authors note as a limitation that interviewer advice was not uniform across participants.
ov-11 · PDF p. 12-12 · PaperLens-generated overview summary (not the authors' words)
These pages discuss a user study of prompt design with a chatbot tool called BotDesigner, focusing on how participants used error labeling and systematic testing. Participants largely stopped labeling errors once they realized the labels were not used by the system, and none used the systematic prompt testing interface, instead declaring victory after a single successful change. The authors describe this as premature victory declaration, similar to overconfident end-user programmers, and note that some professional programmers were more concerned about generalizability. The pages also cover participants struggling to explain why prompts worked or failed across contexts, and a finding that participants favored 'do not' phrasing over the more effective 'do' phrasing, with the authors linking this to early childhood education literature.
Limitation / uncertainty
Limits of these findings
The paper states that the participant pool skewed toward STEM professionals and graduate students and is not representative, and that interviewer advice was not uniform across participants. The authors also list a small early-adopter academic sample and generalization to other populations and non-chatbot domains as open questions.
